- **x-commit**: Git commit with proper message formatting. Invoke ONLY when the user has explicitly asked you to commit—NOT proactively because work feels complete, because you are wrapping up a task, or because committing would be the natural next step. When work feels done, summarize what you did and ASK whether to commit; wait for an explicit go-ahead. The user values that pause: it is where they say 'oh, but also include X', 'fold this into the last commit instead', or 'one more thing first'. Once the user HAS asked for a commit, this skill is mandatory: never run `git commit` without loading it first.
